- **poll-agent-permission-ceiling**: auspol-tracker — every poll-agent caller workflow (twelve since 2026-09-25, Roy Morgan's included) must keep its top-level `permissions:` block at least as wide as what the reusable poll-agent.yml's update job requests (currently contents write + actions read). A one-sided widening breaks EVERY data pipeline at its next scheduled trigger with "Invalid workflow file" startup failures. Pinned by .build/test-workflows.mjs. Worked incident/fixes 0c3a2ba (2026-09-06). ([joshborgnolo/auspol-tracker](https://github.com/joshborgnolo/auspol-tracker/tree/HEAD/.matilda/skills/auto-skill-poll-agent-permission-ceiling))

- **poll-and-coordinate**: The full pause/poll/act/rearm lifecycle for coordination work: pause any armed wake-up so cycles never overlap, load a durable cursor, fetch shared refs, inspect coordination records/MRs/issues/pipelines from the fetched state (not a stale local checkout or a same-SHA duplicate pipeline), classify findings, act within role/gate authority or record the concrete blocker, persist the cursor, then rearm or stop. - **poll-as-reviewer**: Poll one or more GitHub / GHES pull requests that the user is reviewing, for a set time (default one hour), and re-review when the developer pushes new work. Each push (debounced, so a burst of commits is one event) triggers a code review of the new head with the same method as the user's original review - a single-agent review or a multi-agent review - checking every earlier finding against the new head and the developer's replies, plus the new delta. If the original method cannot be established, ask the user; if the user has said they will be unavailable, default to a single-agent review. - **poll-as-worker**: Poll one or more GitHub / GHES pull requests that the user raised, for a set time (default one hour), and act when reviewers respond. New review comments or change requests are batched across every watched PR and taken through a verify-recommend-reply feedback flow; an approval ends the poll for that PR and is only reported. Attended (the default), the user approves every recommendation and reply. - **poll-public-post-replies-incrementally-010ab471ef7dd42b**: # Poll one post for new replies 1. Keep the last reply timestamp observed for each post. 2. Request `GET /api/v1/posts/{post_id}/replies?since=&lt;RFC3339 timestamp&gt;&limit=100` to read visible replies created at or after that time. 3. Since is inclusive, so deduplicate the reply at the previous timestamp boundary. 4. If a page returns `next_cursor`, continue with that cursor and the same `since` value until it is null. 5. Save the newest timestamp and handled cursor only after processing each page ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/poll-public-post-replies-incrementally-010ab471ef7dd42b))
